| BV (2.0) = 222<ref name="TOp382">''Tactical Operations'', p. 382, "Inner Sphere Weapons and Equipment BV Table [Addendum]"</ref>
}}

The '''Binary Laser Cannon''' was a laser weapon previously considered ineffectual, but began to be reconsidered following the reintroduction of double heat sinks.

==Description==
Binary laser weapons, nicknamed '''Blazers''', have existed in [[Blazer Rifle|Rifle]] and [[Blazer Pistol|Pistol]] form for some time, but the 'Mech-scale Binary Laser Cannon was widely considered a dead-end technology. Taking two [[large laser]] cores and fusing them together, the Blazer was designed to fire both cores at the same time, increasing damage penetration. In practice, damage yield proved low compared to the lasers firing separately, and the high heat generated by the Blazer severely taxed available heat sink technology of the time.

While research on the Blazer started in the [[Lyran Commonwealth]] in [[2801]], actual prototype models first showed up in the [[Free Worlds League]] in [[2812]].

With the reintroduction of [[double heat sinks]], the Blazer cannon is now a viable weapon.

==Notes==
Earlier runs of ''[[Tactical Operations]]'' contained an error that switched the heat and damage values of the Binary Laser Cannon and gave an incorrect [[Battle Value]] 2 rating. The correct stats are: Damage (12), Heat (16), BV2 = 222.<ref name=errata/>

## Weapon: Bombast Laser
### Data Extract Profile
{{Update Needed|[[BattleTech Core Rulebook]]}}
{{InfoBoxWeapon

| Year Availability = [[3064]]<ref name="TOp406+"/>
| Heat = Various
| Damage = 7-12
| Short Range = 1-5<ref name="TOp406+">''Tactical Operations'', pp. 406-407, "Heavy Weapons And Equipment Construction Data"</ref>
| Medium Range = 6-10<ref name="TOp406+"/>
| Long Range = 11-15<ref name="TOp406+"/>
| Tons = 7<ref name="TOp406+"/>
| Critical Slots = 3
| Cost (unloaded) = 200,000<ref name="TOp406+"/>
| BV (2.0) = 137<ref name="TOp382">''Tactical Operations'', p. 382, "Inner Sphere Weapons And Equipment BV Table"</ref>
}}

==Description==
Developed by the [[Lyran Alliance]] in  [[3064]], the '''Bombast Laser''' was designed to be the ultimate word in [[laser]] weapons.  During the design process, its engineers had to reduce the weight of the weapon. This sacrifice came from the power supply, which made the weapon slow at charging. 

To make up for this, the engineers added the ability for the warrior to adjust the yield of damage inflicted by the weapon.  The power management, however, proved to be impractical and distracting in the field for its users.  The weapon reached prototype production by 3064.  Its unusual glow from building up its power became a popular visual effect on [[Solaris VII]], making the weapon popular as well.<ref name="TOp320">''Tactical Operations'', p. 320, "Bombast Laser"</ref>

## Weapon: Centurion Weapon System
### Data Extract Profile

}}

==Description==
The eventually inspiration for [[Dark Age era]] [[TSEMP|TSEMP systems]], the '''Centurion Weapon System''' was a [[Star League era]] directed energy weapon firing a focused [[w:Electromagnetic pulse|EMP]] burst and has the ability to temporarily shut down any electronics primarily to non-lethally disable [[BattleMech]]s, [[combat vehicle]]s and [[aerospace fighter]]s.<ref name=JCIP2p127/><ref name=IOp85>''Interstellar Operations'', pp. 85–86: "Alternate Eras: Units and Equipment - Centurion Weapon System (Star League Era)"</ref>

==Overview==
In the late [[twenty-eighth century]], the increasing spread of advanced [[Terran Hegemony]] military technologies to the general [[Inner Sphere]] and the [[Periphery]] led [[Star League Defense Force]] [[General]]s [[Aleksandr Kerensky]] and [[Aaron DeChavilier]] to task researchers with developing non-lethal weapons to disable such "stolen" equipment. A few concepts were reviewed before research focused on the development of an energy weapon intended to produce a localized electromagnetic pulse on impact intended to overload the electronic systems of the target.<ref name=JCIP2p127/><ref name=IOp85/>

Despite this laudable goal, practical testing and combat simulations quickly showed even minimal electronics hardening and simple combat maneuvering rendered the depressingly short-ranged and overly complicated system ineffective against all but the slowest and oldest equipment still in use. During trials engineers fitted test units with special transponders that would automatically shut down units if struck with Centurion beam at increased ranges, with the SLDF agreeing to an extended test in response, but even with this boost the weapon system still proved both too limited and unreliable. Ultimately the system was shelved when the additional transponders were modified to be able to shut down 'Mechs or vehicles by coded radio command, before even they were removed when the codes were leaked far and wide.<ref name=JCIP2p127/><ref name=IOp85/>

Though development of the Centurion system and even the transponders quickly ended and the technology disappeared during the [[LosTech]] period of the [[Succession War era]], the system made a surprising return during the [[Jihad]] with copies of the prototype emerging as part of the "[[Retrotech]]" craze that swept the [[Inner Sphere]]. The [[Word of Blake]] championed these efforts as part of a search for an improved relative of the [[BattleMech Taser]], and while the Blakists never succeeded before their defeat, their research would ultimately allow the [[Republic of the Sphere]] to refine and introduce the TSEMP systems which finally achieve what the Centurion Weapon System could not.<ref name="XTRO:Pp13">''Experimental Technical Readout: Periphery'', p. 13: "TRB-XTS Thunderbird"</ref><ref name=IOp85/><ref name=ER3145p121>''Era Report: 3145'', p. 121</ref><ref name=FM3145p245>''Field Manual: 3145'', p. 245</ref>

==Notes==
The ranges listed reflect if a target is susceptible or resistant to the Centurion Weapon System. Most undamaged military equipment are resistant to the CWS use the shorter [[Small Laser]] ranges, but equipment that is either primitive/retrotech, damaged or older than 150 years old as of 2762 or are fitted with the testing transponders are automatically susceptible. Infantry, including [[battle armor]], and aerospace units larger than 200 tons are automatically immune from the effects.<ref name=IOp85/>

==Canonicity==
This weapon was featured in an official [[BattleTech]] product, namely the ''[[BattleTech (Video Game)|BattleTech]]'' computer game by [[HBS]].

By virtue of being an official albeit not positively canonical product, the game expansion and the weapons and equipment presented therein including this one must be considered [[apocryphal]].
==Description==
The '''COIL''', short for '''C'''ontained '''O'''verflow '''I'''nertia '''L'''inkage is a weapon that generates power from the kinetic energy of a [[BattleMech]]'s movement.  A COIL weapon deals more damage the faster its carrier moves before firing it.

Jump-based movement, as it doesn't involve the 'Mech's limbs moving and generating kinetic energy, is not able to fuel a COIL's shot.

The system can explode if damaged due to the energy stored in its batteries.<ref name=BTHM>"BattleTech: Heavy Metal Expansion Pack"</ref>

==Models==
=== Herakleion ===
This model of COIL-L was recovered from the derelict [[JumpShip]] the ''[[Dobrev (Individual JumpShip)|Dobrev]]''.<ref name=BTHM/>

==Description==
Developed by [[Clan Wolverine]] during the early years of the [[Clans]], the '''Enhanced Extended-Range Particle Projector Cannon''' or '''Enhanced ER PPC''' was something of a midway point between the original [[ER PPC]] and the eventual [[Clan]] model. It had a damage threshold 20% higher than that of the original ER PPC, but the two were otherwise identical. It was popularly used on the ''[[Pulverizer]]'' [[BattleMech]].<ref name=TRGCp40>''Technical Readout: Golden Century'', p. 40: "PUL-3R ''Pulverizer''"</ref>

==Description==
Introduced in [[2620]] by the [[Terran Hegemony]]. The '''Extended-Range Large Laser''' (ER Large Laser) focuses a powerful beam of light on targets that causes damage by overheating or melting the target.<ref name="TMp226"/>

As the name suggests, the Extended-Range variant of the [[Large Laser]] increases the power of the beam to project it to much farther ranges. The power necessary to facilitate the production of such generates a greatly increased quantity of waste heat.

As with most Clan energy weapons, the Clans lack their own standard Large Laser.

*;ER-LL-P: The [[Federated Commonwealth]] employed experimental versions of the ER Large Laser among some of its elite units during the [[War of 3039]].  While achieving the same range and destructive power, these prototypes were less accurate and generated random heat spikes.

===Apocryphal Variants===
{{ApocryphalContentStart}}
These variants were introduced in various apocryphal sources, and thus far have not appeared in any canonical media.

''From [[BattleTech (Video Game)]]:''
*;"SCI" : A LosTech ER Large Laser found in this game listed with an unknown manufacturer 'SCI.' Stats-wise it is similar to the extinct Terran Hegemony ER Large Laser in Classic BattleTech.<ref name=BTVG>''BattleTech'' (video game), Equipment</ref>
{{ApocryphalContentEnd}}

## Weapon: Extended-Range Large Pulse Laser
### Data Extract Profile
{{InfoBoxWeapon
| image = [[File:CERLPSL.png|191px]]
| name = ER Large Pulse Laser
| Type = Energy(Pulse)
| Tech Base = [[Clan]]<ref name="TOp406+"/>
| Year Availability = [[3057]]<ref name="TOp406+"/>
| Heat = 13<ref name="TOp406+">''Tactical Operations'', pp. 406-407, "Heavy Weapons And Equipment Construction Data"</ref>
| Damage = 10<ref name="TOp406+"/>
| Short Range = 1-7<ref name="TOp406+"/>
| Medium Range = 8-15<ref name="TOp406+"/>
| Long Range = 16-23<ref name="TOp406+"/>
| Tons = 6
| Critical Slots = 3
| Cost (unloaded) = 400,000<ref name="TOp406+"/>
| BV (2.0) = 272
}}

==Description==
The '''Extended-Range Large Pulse Laser''' is the largest ER pulse laser created by the scientists of [[Clan Wolf]]. Inflicting as much damage as a standard [[PPC]], the pulse laser offers the warrior using it a better chance of hitting a target. Unfortunately, the longer range of the weapon slightly reduces its accuracy when compared to traditional pulse laser weapons.<ref name="TOp320">''Tactical Operations'', p. 320, "ER Pulse Laser"</ref>

Though the ER pulse laser series reached the advanced prototype stage in [[3057]], the events of the [[Refusal War]] and the formation of [[Clan Wolf-in-Exile]] prevented them from reaching production. Clan Wolf lost many of the developers to Clan Wolf-in-Exile, and the Exiled Wolves were too busy trying to survive to begin production of these weapons.

==References==
<references/>

## Weapon: Extended-Range Medium Laser
### Data Extract Profile
{{InfoBoxWeapon

| Type = Energy (Direct-Fire)

| Year Availability =

IS = [[3058]] ([[Free Worlds League|FWL]])<ref name="TMp226"/>
| Heat = 5<ref name="TMp341">''TechManual'', p. 341: "Inner Sphere Weapons And Equipment Table"</ref><ref name="TMp343">''TechManual'', p. 343: "Clan Weapons And Equipment Table"</ref>

IS = 62<ref name="TMp317">''TechManual'', p. 317: "Inner Sphere Weapons and Equipment BV Table"</ref>
}}

==Description==
The '''extended-range medium laser''' ('''ER medium laser''') focuses a powerful beam of light on targets that causes damage by melting or shattering armor off of the target.<ref name="TMp226"/>

As the name suggests, the Extended-Range variant of the [[medium laser]] improves the focusing system of the laser to project it to much farther ranges. The optics are less efficient, though, necessitating more input power for the same armor ablation and thus more waste heat in the weapon.

==Variants==
In the years between the [[Second Exodus]] and the beginning of [[Operation KLONDIKE]] the nascent [[Clan]]s began development of what would become the ER medium laser. One of the first of the advanced weapon types to be developed, experimental prototype versions of this new weapon were used during Operation KLONDIKE in small quantities. The Clan Prototype ER medium laser (ER-ML-CP) available during this period has identical stats to the Inner Sphere ER medium laser, other than that it weighs 1.5 tons.<ref name="H:OKp156">''Historical: Operation KLONDIKE'', p. 156: "Prototype Clan Systems"</ref>

==Description==
The '''Extended-Range Medium Pulse Laser''' is the middleweight ER pulse Laser created by the scientists of [[Clan Wolf]]. Like all pulse lasers, it offers the warrior using it a better chance of hitting a target. Unfortunately, the longer range of the weapon slightly reduces its accuracy when compared to traditional pulse laser weapons.<ref name="TOp320">''Tactical Operations'', p. 320, "ER Pulse Laser"</ref> An ER Medium Pulse Laser inflicts the same amount of damage as a typical [[medium pulse laser]], but at a slightly longer range and with a moderate heat increase.

Though the ER pulse laser series reached the advanced prototype stage in [[3057]], the events of the [[Refusal War]] and the formation of [[Clan Wolf-in-Exile]] prevented them from reaching production. Clan Wolf lost many of the developers to Clan Wolf-in-Exile, and the Exiled Wolves were too busy trying to survive to begin production of these weapons.

==Description==
The '''Extended-Range Micro Laser''' is a very compact version of conventional ER laser weaponry. This laser weapon was specifically developed by [[Clan Smoke Jaguar]] for use on very light combat units, such as their [[Elemental|Elementals]] and [[ProtoMech]]s. The extended-range micro laser lacks widespread use, and the [[Inner Sphere]] has seen no need to develop their own version.<ref name="TMp226"/>

==Description==
First introduced in [[2751]] by the [[Terran Hegemony]], the '''Extended-Range Particle Projector Cannon''', or '''ER PPC''' for short, has a greatly increased range than that of a standard [[Particle Projector Cannon|PPC]]. Capable of causing extreme damage at long range, it unfortunately generates fifty percent higher heat than the standard weapon, so can be difficult to use effectively. The ER PPC is also noteworthy for having no minimal range.<ref name=TMp233>''TechManual'', pp. 233-234: "Extended Range (ER) PPC"</ref>

After becoming [[lostech]] during the [[Succession Wars (History)|Succession Wars]], the ER PPC was first reintroduced to the [[Inner Sphere]] in [[3037]] by the [[Draconis Combine]]. By this point the [[Clans]] had greatly refined the weapon, with slight reductions in size and weight, but most notably boosting its damage by fifty percent, making it capable of shaving off almost a ton of armor in a single hit.<ref name=TMp233/> Despite the best efforts of the [[Great Houses]] during the initial Clan Invasion period, none have been able to successfully replicate the improvements of the invaders, though these efforts have resulted in the [[Heavy PPC]] which matches the thermo-kinetic damage potential (and heat) of the Clan weapon albeit without the long reach, and even greater weight.<ref name=TMp234>''TechManual'', p. 234: "Heavy PPC"</ref> Similar research has also led to devices such as the [[PPC Capacitor]] that can emulate the damage increase and range when coupled with an Inner Sphere ER PPC, but at the cost of even greater strain on a BattleMech's cooling systems.<ref name=MTp92>''Maximum Tech'', p. 92: "PPC Capacitor"</ref>

===Fusigon Longtooth===
The Fusigon Longtooth is a model of ER PPC common to the [[Free Worlds League]]. In the wake of the [[Outreach Summit]], 90% of the Fusigon Longtooth ER PPCs manufactured at Campbelton were exported to the [[Federated Commonwealth]], triggering a resumption of the League's near chronic shortage of PPCs during the early stages of the Clan Invasion. The situation was made even more galling given that many of these cannons were used on the ''Griffin''s manufactured at the [[Brigadier Corporation]] plant on [[Oliver]] taken from the League during the [[Fourth Succession War]]. By [[3054]] Fusigon's workers were especially outspoken on the matter, responding with riots and strikes, attempts to deceive FedCom officials about production volumes and sabotage of exported components, incidents that [[Captain-General of the Free Worlds League|Captain-General]] [[Thomas Halas|Thomas Marik]] had largely chosen to ignore.<ref name=ORp121>''Objective Raids'', p. 121: "Campbelton - Fusigon Heavy Weaponry"</ref>

===Kinslaughter H-Class===
Developed by Martinson Armaments for their SPT-N2 ''Spartan'', the Kinslaughter H-Class was considered the apex of the ER PPC during the [[Star League era]],<ref name=TM-234>''Technical Manual'', p. 234: "Weapons and Heavy Equipment - Particle Projector Cannon (PPC) - ER PPC"</ref> noted for its inclusion of special insulating cones that made the weapon both easier to maintain and ensured it generated less heat than many other models of ER PPCs.<ref name=RI-31>''Rhonda's Irregulars'', p. 31: "Recovered Star League Equipment - SPT-N2 Spartan"</ref> During the Clan Invasion era only [[ComStar]] and the [[Word of Blake]] were capable of building the Kinslaughter H-Class.<ref name=TRO3058-136>''Technical Readout: 3058'', p. 136: "SPT-N2 Spartan"</ref>

===Type DDS "Kingston"===
The Type DDS "Kingston" Extended-Range Particle Projection Cannon is one of the more modern and common types of Clan-spec ER PPCs in Clan and the Inner Sphere space following the FedCom Civil War. Debuted by [[Clan Cloud Cobra]] around [[3059]] in time for use on their new [[BattleMech#Quadrupedal|Four-Legged BattleMech]] the ''Stalking Spider'',<ref name=TRO3060R-159/> [[Clan Star Adder]] and [[Clan Diamond Shark]] would secure the designs for the weapon for use on updated Phoenix updates of the venerable ''Glass Spider''<ref name=TRO3055U-116/> and ''Warhammer IIC''<ref name=TROPP-70/> manufactured in the [[Clan Homeworlds]]. The Diamond Sharks' forced evacuation from the Homeworlds would also result in the start of manufacture of the weapon in the Inner Sphere as well,<ref name=TROP-198/> which the mercantile Clan Sea Fox would offer to Clan and Inner Sphere buyers such as the [[Capellan Confederation]]<ref name=TR3145CC-30>''Technical Readout: 3145 Capellan Confederation'', p. 30: "Yinghuochong"</ref> and possibly the [[Federated Suns]].<ref name="TRO3145:FSp44">''Technical Readout: 3145: Federated Suns'', p. 44: "BLK-NT-5H Black Knight"</ref>

### Data Extract Profile

==Description==
The '''Extended-Range Small Laser''' ('''ER Small Laser''') focuses a powerful beam of light on targets that causes damage by overheating or melting the target.

As the name suggests, the Extended-Range variant of the [[Small Laser]] increases the power of the beam to project it to much farther ranges. The power necessary to facilitate the production of such generates an increased quantity of waste heat.<ref name="TMp226"/>

Extended range small lasers can be mounted on [[infantry]], [[battle armor|powered armor]], or [[Elemental|Elementals]]. <!-- Removed reference to "unlike larger models" because some battle armor models mount ER Medium Lasers; removed reference to the Clans not having a standard small laser as that's not relevant to an article on the ER Small Laser. -->

==Variants==
In the years between the [[Second Exodus]] and the beginning of [[Operation KLONDIKE]] the nascent [[Clan]]s began development of what would become the ER Small Laser. One of the first of the advanced weapon types to be developed, experimental prototype versions of this new weapon were used during Operation KLONDIKE in small quantities. The Clan Prototype ER Small Laser (ER-SL-CP) available during this period has identical stats to the Inner Sphere ER Small Laser, other than that it generates 2 heat per shot.<ref name="H:OKp156">''Historical: Operation KLONDIKE'', p. 156: "Prototype Clan Systems"</ref>

## Weapon: Extended-Range Small Pulse Laser
### Data Extract Profile
{{InfoBoxWeapon
| image = [[File:CERSMPSL.png|191px]]
| name = ER small pulse laser
| Type = Energy Weapon
| Tech Base = [[Clan]]<ref name="TOp406+">''Tactical Operations'', pp. 406-407, "Heavy Weapons And Equipment Construction Data"</ref>
| Year Availability = [[3057]]<ref name="TOp406+"/>
| Heat = 3<ref name="TOp406+"/>
| Damage = 5<ref name="TOp406+"/>
| Minimum Range = N/A
| Short Range = 1-2<ref name="TOp406+"/>
| Medium Range = 3-4<ref name="TOp406+"/>
| Long Range = 5-6<ref name="TOp406+"/>
| Tons = 1.5
| Critical Slots = 1
| Ammo Per Ton = N/A
| Cost (unloaded) = 30,000<ref name="TOp406+"/>
| Ammo Cost (per ton) = N/A
| BV (1.0) = N/A
| BV (2.0) = 36
}}

==Description==
The '''Extended-Range Small Pulse Laser''' (also referred to as the '''ER small pulse laser''') is the smallest ER pulse laser created by the scientists of [[Clan Wolf]]. Like the [[small pulse laser|weapon that inspired it]], the pulse laser offers the warrior using it a better chance of hitting a target. Unfortunately, the longer range of the weapon slightly reduces its accuracy when compared to traditional pulse laser weapons.<ref name="TOp320">''Tactical Operations'', p. 320, "ER Pulse Laser"</ref>

The ER small pulse laser was likely the first weapon developed in its family, as small lasers are cheaper than their larger cousins. Although the weapon inflicts as much damage as a standard [[medium laser]], the ER small pulse laser's range isn't significantly better than the [[small pulse laser]] or [[ER small laser]]s already in use with the [[Clans]]. In fact, an [[OmniMech]] could mount three ER small lasers for every ER small pulse laser and inflict more damage for the same heat cost. The only offsetting advantage is the fact that the ER small pulse laser would be slightly easier to aim.

Though the ER pulse laser series reached the advanced prototype stage in [[3057]], the events of the [[Refusal War]] and the formation of [[Clan Wolf-in-Exile]] prevented them from reaching production. Clan Wolf lost many of the developers to Clan Wolf-in-Exile, and the Exiled Wolves were too busy trying to survive to begin production of these weapons.

==Description==
Introduced in [[2025]], the standard '''Flamer''' taps into a [[BattleMech]]'s reactor to produce heat in the form of a plasma release.<ref name=TMp218>''TechManual'', p. 218</ref>  An extremely short-ranged weapon, the flamer is devastating against [[infantry]], however damage done against other 'Mechs and vehicles is negligible, though it can raise the enemy unit's heat levels. The flamer is also often used to set ambient objects such as trees aflame, making it useful for burning forests or cities in order to slow the enemy down or cover friendly movements. A clear example of such weapon usage in a 'Mech is the ''[[Firestarter (BattleMech)|Firestarter]]'' BattleMech.

===Olympian===
The Olympian Flamer was an older design using a napalm-based fuel mixture instead of directly tapping the [[fusion engine]]'s plasma field. The napalm gel is forced along pressure hoses towards the nozzle where it is mixed with small amounts of phosphorus suspended in water; when the phosphorus mixes with air it ignites. In addition to jettison racks for the napalm canisters this makes the flamer amongst the safest devised, since the fuel is stored far from the ignition point and only a small amount of phosphorus is needed.<ref name=TRO2750/><ref name=TRO3025r>''Technical Readout: 3025 Revised'', pp. 14–15</ref><ref name=TRO3039>''Technical Readout: 3039'', pp. 250–251</ref>

==Related Weapons==
* The [[vehicular flamer]] is a less advanced version of the flamer and can operate on vehicles and 'Mechs that are powered by [[ICE|internal combustion engines]] and [[Fuel cell|hydrogen fuel cells]], by using volatile fuel as an ammunition source. Besides being usable by such low-cost units, combined with its own very low price point, it lacks any advantages on the modern battlefield and should probably be avoided on 'Mechs which run on fusion engines.
* The [[fluid gun]] (and its weaker civilian-grade cousin, the [[sprayer]]) could be argued to be a more versatile evolution of the vehicular flamer, since by being loaded with the right fuel it could easily serve that function.
* The [[ER flamer]] is a more advanced version of the flamer, sacrificing some of its effectiveness at raising enemy heat levels in exchange for a longer range, which could make it useful in some situations.
* The [[heavy flamer]] is, quite simply, a more powerful version of the flamer, with both the advantages and disadvantages that might imply.
* Both the [[plasma rifle]] and [[plasma cannon]] could probably be considered to be the pinnacle of heat-inducing weaponry, but they are also both fairly late to the game.
* [[Inferno (Missile)|Inferno]] missiles serve a very similar function and role to flamers, except that the heat is delivered to its customer by means of a guided, rocket-propelled warhead; the downside is that the raw explosive damage potential of the missile had to be sacrificed.
* [[Incendiary]] munitions for autocannons were invented by the [[New Avalon Institute of Science]], but they don't have any effect against BattleMechs in their current form. Not to be confused with [[incendiary ammo]] for personal weapons such as handguns and crossbows.

==Description==
Introduced in [[3059]], [[Clan Star Adder]] developed the Heavy Lasers as a way of packing more punch into a relatively small package. All Heavy Lasers, however, build up a tremendous amount of heat, have a subtle firing delay  and potentially interfere with a [['Mech]]'s sensors meaning the weapon suffers from poor accuracy.<ref name=TMp226>''TechManual'', p. 226: "Heavy Lasers"</ref><ref name=BT:MRp128>''BattleTech Master Rules'', p. 128: "Heavy Lasers"</ref>

}}

==Description==
The '''Heavy PPC''' is a larger, more potent version of the [[Particle Projector Cannon]]. The Heavy PPC has the same damage potential and heat buildup of a [[Clan]] [[ER PPC]].  However, it lacks the range of that weapon, as well as possessing greatly increased weight and bulk.<ref name=TMp234/>

Developed alongside the [[Light PPC|Light]] and [[Snub-Nose PPC]]s in [[3067]] by the [[Draconis Combine]] in the final months of the [[Second Star League]], as part of a secret technology trade deal between the Combine and the [[Free Worlds League]], one that [[Coordinator of the Draconis Combine|Coordinator]] [[Theodore Kurita (31st c.)|Theodore Kurita]] was apparently aware and even ordered it be shielded from the [[Star League Intelligence Command]], the Heavy PPC had already begun to spread throughout the [[Inner Sphere]] as the [[Jihad]] broke out. Being used on both sides of the conflict with the [[Word of Blake]] would cement the utility of the Heavy PPC.<ref name=TMp234/>

===Lord's Light 3===

Designed by: Unknown<!-- Possibly [[Alshain Weapons]] on [[Tok Do]]--> in the [[Draconis Combine]]<ref name="TRO3075p70-71"/><ref name="TRJp66-67"/><ref name="TRO3085p242-243"/>

The first known production combat unit to be equipped with the Lord's Light 3 was the WGT-1LAW/SC ''[[Wight]]'' introduced in [[3069]]<ref name="WGT-1LAW/SC">[http://www.masterunitlist.info/Unit/Details/3546/wight-wgt-1lawsc Wight WGT-1LAW/SC - Master Unit List]</ref> for the [[Draconis Combine]],<ref name="TRO3075p70-71"/> although [[Alshain Weapons]], the first known manufacturer of Lord's Light PPC weapons (in [[2759]])<ref name=TRO3025p30>{{cite|title=Technical Readout: 3025|page=30|year=1986}}: "PNT-9R ''Panther''"</ref><ref name=PNT-9R>[http://www.masterunitlist.info/Unit/Details/2425/panther-pnt-9r Panther PNT-9R - Master Unit List]</ref> was shipping an unspecified model of Heavy PPC for the AWS-10KM ''[[Awesome#Variants|Awesome]]'' for the Draconis Combine and [[Free Worlds League]]<ref name=TRO3050up84>{{cite|title=Technical Readout: 3050 Upgrade|page=84|year=2007}}: "AWS-9M ''Awesome''" Variants</ref><ref name="HB:HMp179">{{cite|title=Handbook: House Marik|page=179|year=2015}}: "Irian Industries": "...a technician at Irian BattleMechs Unlimited of Irian oversees the final installation of the Draconis Combine's newest weapon system (the Heavy PPC) on a new Awesome variant (AWS-10KM) that will see joint deployment to Combine and League forces."</ref> a year earlier in [[3068]].<ref name=AWS-10KM>[http://www.masterunitlist.info/Unit/Details/172/awesome-aws-10km Awesome AWS-10KM - Master Unit List]</ref>

The Combine would provide the specs for the Lord Light 3 to [[Ghost Bear Dominion]]-headquartered [[Odin Manufacturing]] in the wake of the Jihad, in return securing for the DCMS ten-percent of the production of the modernized SHD-12C ''Shadow Hawk'' that was introduced in [[3079]]<ref name=SHD-12C>[http://www.masterunitlist.info/Unit/Details/2897/shadow-hawk-shd-12c Shadow Hawk SHD-12C - Master Unit List]</ref> equipped with the weapon.<ref name="TRO3085p242-243"/>

===Magna Hellfire===

Designed by: [[Magna Metals Inc]]<ref name=H:HMp133>{{cite|title=Handbook: House Marik|page=133|chapter=Magna Metals Inc (MagMet)|year=2015}}</ref> on [[Lopez]],{{ref label|Hellfire|J:FR}} in the [[Duchy of Andurien]]

The first known production combat unit to be equipped with the Magna Hellfire was the [[Warlord (BattleMech)|BLR-2D ''Warlord'']] introduced in [[3070]] for the [[Federated Suns]].<ref name=BLR-2D>[http://www.masterunitlist.info/Unit/Details/3509/warlord-blr-2d Warlord BLR-2D - Master Unit List]</ref>

==Notes==
:'''J:FR'''.{{note|Hellfire}}{{note|Starflare}}{{note|Supernova}} Lopez is a dead or dying world as of [[3081]]<ref name="J:FRp63">{{cite|chapter=WHAT OF LOPEZ? |title=Jihad: Final Reckoning|year=2011|pages=22, 61, 63|quote-page=22|quote=Oriente [LNN] – At 0132 Terran Standard Time, 12 October 3080, the world of Lopez effectively ceased to exist.}}</ref>
:'''DI'''.{{note|DI}} Per Shrapnel #10, "By 3110...Defiance stopped supplying components to what had become a competitor, forcing Nashan to cease production of the promising BattleMech."<ref name="Shrapnel10: Hel"/> So this 'Mech's Heavy PPC would have actually been manufactured by Defiance Industries, which had a plant on [[Furillo]], and was known to have produced the Defiance 980 on [[Hesperus II]].<ref name=TRO3145LCp18/> [[Nashan Diversified|Nashan]] thus only did final assembly using that component on their HL-1 ''[[Eris#Variants|Hel]]''.

==Description==
Introduced in [[3059]] by [[Clan Star Adder]] developed the heavy lasers to deal more damage, but the side effect of that was increased heat output. In addition, a subtle firing delay and interference with a [[BattleMech]]'s sensors means that the weapon suffers from poor accuracy.<ref name="TMp226">''TechManual'', p. 226, "Lasers"</ref>

## Weapon: Heavy Sub-Capital Laser
### Data Extract Profile
{{InfoBoxWeapon
| image = [[Image:Sub-Capital Laser.jpg|191px]]
| name = Heavy Sub-Capital Laser
| Type = Capital Direct Energy
| Tech Base = [[Inner Sphere]]<ref name="TOp410+"/>
| Year Availability = [[3073]]<ref name="TOp410+"/> ([[Word of Blake|WoB]])<ref name="TOp343">''Tactical Operations'', p. 343</ref>
| Heat = 32<ref name="TOp410+">''Tactical Operations'', pp. 410-411: "Heavy Weapons And Equipment Combat Data/Construction Data"</ref>
| Damage = 3 (Capital)<ref name="TOp410+"/><br>30 (Standard)<ref name="TOp410+"/>
| Minimum Range = N/A
| Short Range = 1-7
| Medium Range = 8-13
| Long Range = 14-18
| Extreme Range = 19-22
| Tons = 250<ref name="TOp410+"/>
| Critical Slots = 1-Capital<ref name="TOp410+"/>
| Ammo Per Ton = N/A
| Cost (unloaded) = 450,000<ref name="TOp410+"/>
| Ammo Cost (per ton) = N/A
| BV (1.0) = N/A
| BV (2.0) = 531<ref name="TOp384">''Tactical Operations'', p. 384: "Inner Sphere Capital Scale Weapons And Equipment BV Table"</ref>
}}

==Description==
The '''Heavy Sub-Capital Laser''', also known as the '''SCL/3''' was the largest of the Sub-Capital Lasers produced by the [[Word of Blake]]. Capable of inflicting as much damage as a hit from a [[White Shark]] capital missile, the fact that the Heavy Sub-Capital Laser wasn't reliant upon ammunition was a key selling point. In addition, the SCL/3 could be safely mounted on the planets of the [[Blake Protectorate]] as a defense against attacking [[WarShips]]. Like their capital scale brethren, deploying the SCL/3 in this role didn't run the risk of the defender's weapon fire returning to the planet's surface the way ballistic weapons fire would.<ref name="TOp343"/>

==Notes==
Game rules for the Heavy Sub-Capital Laser can be found on page 343 in ''Tactical Operations''.

==Description==
"Laser" stands for "'''L'''ight '''A'''mplification by '''S'''timulated '''E'''mission of '''R'''adiation", or a device that emits an amplified, contiguous beam of light onto a small surface area. Lasers cause damage by firing an intense beam of light at a target, flooding concentrated energy in the form of heat, which can melt material and overwhelm heat-sensitive electronics. The '''Large Laser''' differs from its smaller cousins by being a [[w:Gamma-ray_laser|gamma-ray laser]] firing a much more powerful beam, allowing it to reach to further ranges and cause more damage.<ref name=ER:2750p99>''Era Report: 2750'', p. 99: "Laser Systems"</ref> However, the increased bulk and heat generation limits the weapon to [[Combat Vehicle|ground vehicles]], aircraft, and [[BattleMech]]s. The type was first introduced by the [[Terran Alliance]] in [[2306]].<ref name="IOp118">''Interstellar Operations'', p. 118: "Prototype Dates for Basic Weapons Table"</ref>

The ubiquitous weapon eventually earned a [[Large Laser (beverage)|cocktail in its name]].<ref name=S21VotS>''Shrapnel Issue 21'': "Voices of the Sphere: What Your Drink Says About You"</ref>

== Models ==
The Large Laser is manufactured on the following planets:
===Amdecker 300===
The Amdecker 300 was one of the less well-known brands of Large Laser produced during the Star League era. Though noted as a highly accurate weapon, numerous ''Rotunda'' drivers claim it also generates waste heat unpredictably, complicating monitoring and adequately venting it. If not properly managed, the heat can damage the Amdecker 300 and its mountings forcing early maintenance, a situation made worse by the fact some technicians have difficulty repairing the weapon.<ref name=TRO2750p90>''Technical Readout: 2750'', p. 90: "Rotunda Vehicle Profile"</ref><ref name=TRO3050Rp194>''Technical Readout: 3050 Revised'', p. 194: "Rotunda Vehicle Profile"</ref><ref name=TRO3050Up154>''Technical Readout: 3050 Upgrade'', p. 154: "Rotunda Vehicle Profile"</ref>

=== BLW Blow Mark III ===
The BLW Blow Mark III is noted as a dependable and durable weapon system even under the most adverse combat conditions. The Blow III was notable for having five power concentration settings, ranging from a wide beam that did little damage but disrupted a target's electronics and tracking systems up to the standard concentrated beam, with the pilot able to control these in combat unlike other laser models which could only be modified by ground crew.<ref name=TRO2750p64>''Technical Readout: 2750'', p. 64: "ZRO-114 Zero"</ref>

===Cyclops Eye===
Developed towards the end of the [[Third Succession War]] primarily for the [[Drillson]], the Cyclops Eye was an unconventional design. While most [[Succession Wars era]] heavy lasers feature vulnerable long barrels and focusing mirrors, this system needed only a small, slit-like emitter.  This was a result of the system's use of a unique combination of laser and [[PPC |particle beam technology]].<ref name="TRO3026">''Technical Readout: 3026'', p. 50</ref><ref name="TRO3026R">''Technical Readout: 3026 Revised'', p. 54</ref><ref name="TRO3039">''Technical Readout: 3039'', p. 62</ref>

Instead of firing beam of light or a stream of particles, the Cyclops Eye used a combination of both to produce a beam of significant penetrating power.<ref name="TRO3026"/><ref name="TRO3026R"/> The effect of this weapon is comparable to the large laser systems produced by [[Diverse Optics]] or [[Martell]]. The Cyclops Eye is less vulnerable to damage than a conventional system, and is also easier to maintain and manufacture.<ref name="TRO3026"/><ref name="TRO3026R"/><ref name="TRO3039"/>

===Lushann Redbeam===
Due to the design engineers focusing on the Redbeam's ability to withstand damage and maintain effectiveness, the Lushann Redbeam is larger in size and more unwieldy than others in the same class. This, in the opinion of the authors of [[StarCorps Industries]]' ''[[Masters and Minions: The StarCorps Dossiers|Notable Players]]'' report, makes it more appropriate to heavy tanks than more mobile [[BattleMechs]].<ref name=M&M>''Masters and Minions: The StarCorps Dossiers'', p. 163: "Lushann Industrials Limited"</ref>

Like all Lushann-manufactured lasers, each Lushann Redbeam is inspected mechanically three times, checked over twice by hand, and tested on both an indoor and outdoor firing range before being released for shipping, a testament to the company's reputation for good quality weaponry.<ref name=OR-164>''Objective Raids'', p. 164: "Lushann Industrials"</ref>

===Nightwind===
An old but extremely reliable weapon, all of the Nightwind's components are battle-tested and quite sturdy, but are so bulky that it is one of the largest military lasers ever built. As a testament to the Nightwind's robustness, on the ''Wyvern'' a pair of [[Starflash]] [[Small Laser]]s are able to draw power directly from the Nightwind's primary feed without any loss in efficiency for any of the weapons.<ref name=TRO2750-28/><ref name=TRO3025R-52/><ref name=TRO3050U-196/>

===Thunderbolt A5M===
Developed by Defiance Industries for the ''Zeus'', the Defiance Thunderbolt A5M Large Laser is an extremely compact weapon. Limited by the available space on chassis of the ''Zeus'', Defiance used their increasingly rare knowledge of fiber optics to dispense with the typical bulky barrel common to most other Large Lasers to design a smaller though no less effective weapon.<ref name=TRO3025r-106>''Technical Readout: 3025 Revised</ref>

Though Defiance was one of the few manufacturers who had the knowledge to work with fiber optics during the [[Succession Wars era]],<ref name=TRO3025r-106/> by the [[Third Succession War]] even they were concerned the Thunderbolt A5M would join the ranks of [[LosTech]] as the conflict dragged on as the weapon's production rate began to slow due to its complexity.<ref name=XTRO:SWp8>''Experimental Technical Readout: Succession Wars, Volume 1'', p. 8</ref>

==Description==
'''Pulse lasers''' differ from traditional laser weaponry, in that instead of firing one powerful beam, they maintain laser beams fired off in quick succession. While offering an overall increased rate of fire, the heat output also increases accordingly. Pulse lasers increase damage because they allow vaporized armor to dissipate from the location of damage. This allows subsequent pulses to reach the target area without being diffused by the vapor.<ref name="TMp226"/> Like all pulse lasers, Large Pulse Lasers are also more accurate than traditional Large Lasers. However, they have a lower effective range.

'''Large Pulse Lasers''' are the largest pulse lasers, with a both destructive and far-reaching pulse beam. While even assault 'Mechs can be devastated by a short barrage of Large Pulse Laser fire, the heat buildup is tremendous and will quickly overheat 'Mechs if used for prolonged periods.<ref name="TMp226"/>

While not designated as such in the names, Clan Pulse Lasers have greatly extended range and deal a bit more damage over Inner Sphere models.

==Description==
The '''Large Re-Engineered Laser''' is an offshoot of experiments to mate a Clan [[Heavy Laser]] with a [[Pulse Laser]]. Though the experiments were unsuccessful, the resulting Re-Engineered Laser family has the unique ability to ignore the effects of [[Ferro-Lamellor]], [[Hardened Armor|Hardened]], and [[Reflective Armor]]. This allows the Large Re-Engineered Laser to inflict its full damage on units protected by those armor types.<ref name="FM:3145p244/>

The Large Re-Engineered Laser is the largest of the Re-Engineered Laser family. Like all Re-Engineered Lasers, as it uses shorter pulses it only receives a -1 to hit modifier.<ref name=errata1/> It is compatible with [[Targeting Computer]]s. Aside from its unique armor-penetrating properties, it behaves like a standard [[Large Laser]].<ref name=IOp89>''Interstellar Operations, p.89 - RE-ENGINEERED LASERS "Updated description of weapon and rules of use."</ref>

==Description==
The '''Large Variable-Speed Pulse Laser''' is the largest of the [[variable-speed pulse laser]]s in use. Weighing almost as much as a [[heavy PPC]], the large variable-speed pulse laser Laser can inflict nearly as much damage on a target, and has a better chance of hitting the target. With these facts in mind, [[Word of Blake]] [[MechWarrior (pilot)|MechWarrior]]s often use them when attacking targets in tight terrain such as cities.

## Weapon: Large X-Pulse Laser
### Data Extract Profile
{{InfoBoxWeapon
| name = Large X-Pulse Laser
| image = [[Image:Large X-Pulse.png]]
| Type = Energy (Direct Fire)
| Tech Base = [[Inner Sphere]]
| Year Availability = [[3057]]<ref name=TO_321>''Tactical Operations'', p. 321, "X-Pulse Lasers"</ref>
| Heat = 14<ref name=TO_408-9/>
| Damage = 9<ref name=TO_408-9/>
| Minimum Range = None<ref name=TO_408-9>''Tactical Operations'', pp. 408-409, "Heavy Weapons and Equipment Combat Data"</ref>
| Short Range = 1-5<ref name=TO_408-9/>
| Medium Range = 6-10<ref name=TO_408-9/>
| Long Range = 11-15<ref name=TO_408-9/>
| Tons = 7<ref name=TO_408-9/>
| Critical Slots = 2<ref name=TO_408-9/>
| Ammo Per Ton = N/A
| Cost (unloaded) = 275,000
| Ammo Cost (per ton) = N/A
| BV (1.0) = n/a
| BV (2.0) = 178<ref name=TO_382>''Tactical Operations'', p. 382, "Inner Sphere Weapons and Equipment BV Table [Addendum]"</ref>}}

==History==
X-Pulse Lasers were an attempt, started in [[3055]], by [[Federated Commonwealth]] technicians to increase the range of [[Pulse Laser|pulse laser]]s. The research endeavor was hampered by the [[FedCom Civil War]], though prototypes existed at the time that had fired faster and with more power than current Inner Sphere pulse lasers. By the time of the [[Jihad]], X-Pulse Lasers were undergoing preproduction testing and presumably fell into the hands of the [[Word of Blake]]. By [[3075]], the technology had not yet been made widely available but had started to hit the markets by [[3078]].<ref name=TO_321/><ref name=TRP_207>''Technical Readout: Prototypes'', p. 207, "Jihad Technology Advancement Table"</ref>

==Description==
X-Pulse Lasers are not a new weapon ''per se'', but an upgrade that is separately added to the weapon for an additional cost. X-Pulse lasers also build up tremendous heat but this has been considered by technicians a necessary drawback. '''Large X-Pulse Lasers''' are the biggest and deadliest of the X-Pulse Lasers and though they build massive amounts of extra heat, they boast the same range as a standard [[Large Laser]].

==Background==
The [[Kurita|Kuritan]] design of the Large X-Pulse Lasers is accredited to [[Buda Weapons]] Chief Researcher [[Hideoshi Yamika]] in [[3073]]. In that year, he smuggled at least three prototypes and his team to [[Luthien]] to battlefield-test the weapons. In March [[3074]], he and his prototypes were captured by the [[Word of Blake]].<ref name=JTPL-10/11>''Jihad Turning Points: Luthien'', pp. 10-11, "Light Machine"</ref>

The '''Laser Anti-Missile System''' is an advanced self-defense weapons system.

==Description==
The laser anti-missile system (LAMS) is an advanced variation of the standard [[anti-missile system]]s fielded by both [[Inner Sphere]] and [[Clan]] militaries. While the standard system utilizes a [[machine gun]] linked to specialized tracking systems, the LAMS uses a [[small pulse laser]], ensuring that the system never runs out of ammunition. While solving the frequent lament of the ammunition dependent standard system, the disadvantage is that even with the strength of each laser pulse reduced to the minimum required to destroy missiles, the heat buildup is much more significant.<ref name=TO-22>''Tactical Operations'', p. 322: "Laser Anti-Missile System"</ref>

First researched by [[Clan Wolf]] in [[3045]], their version of the laser anti-missile system entered the prototype production phase in [[3048]]<ref name=TO-22/> and finally starting standard production in [[3079]] with the Clan LAMS becoming widely and commonly available to all the surviving Clans by [[3145]].<ref name=IO-35>''Interstellar Operations'', p. 35: "Universal Technology Advancement Table"</ref>

The Inner Sphere version did not lag far behind; stemming from the R&D efforts of the then-[[Federated Commonwealth]] in [[3054]], the [[Federated Suns]] started prototype design and production in [[3059]].<ref name=TO-22/> The Inner Sphere laser anti-missile system finally entering standard production in [[3079]] and becoming commonly available among all the Inner Sphere powers by [[3145]] at the same time as the Clan version.<ref name=IO-35/> Some evidence exists that [[Firmir Weaponry]] of [[Betelgeuse]] was also developing similar systems around same time as FedCom researchers.<ref name=TH-68>''Tactical Handbook'', pp. 68–69: "Defensive Armaments -  Laser Anti-Missile System"</ref>

The LAMS can be mounted both on [[BattleMechs]] and [[Combat Vehicle|vehicles]]. As its heat buildup is high, the unit with a LAMS must carry enough [[heat sinks]] to counter the higher output it produces and [[internal combustion engine]]s require [[power amplifier]]s equal to the task.<ref name=TO-22/>

## Weapon: Laser Pulse Module
### Data Extract Profile

The first '''Laser Pulse Module''' was introduced by the [[Republic Institute of Strategic Combat]] in [[3137]].<ref name=iop45>''Interstellar Operations'', p. 45</ref>

= Description =
Developed by the Republic Institute of Strategic Combat just shortly prior to the infamous Black Out, the '''Laser Pulse Module''' is designed to be fitted on any Standard or ER Laser, giving it an alternate fire mode which increases the heat output but increases the accuracy along the same lines as a [[Pulse Laser]]. Though promising, it never reached production status and thus only prototypes ever made it into the field. These only offered a small improvement to standard lasers at the cost of increased heat and possible catastrophic failure of the module and the laser it was attached to.<ref name=iop93>''Interstellar Operations'', p. 93</ref>
 
= Construction and Game Rules =
A Laser Pulse Module can be attached to an ER or standard laser of any size, as long as it has an [[Inner Sphere]] technology base. It can be used by any combat unit other than battle armor and conventional infantry. A Laser Pulse Module can only modify one single laser, and a laser can only have one LPM attached to it. The LPM must be placed in the same location as the laser it modifies; this can affect turret weight of vehicles and it does affect the size of [[Targeting Computer]]s. It weighs one ton and occupies a single critical hit slot.<ref name=iop93/>

In combat, the LPM-modified laser can operate in its standard mode or operate as a pulse laser. Standard mode uses the laser's standard range, damage, and attack modifiers. When the LPM is activated to use pulse mode, the standard range and damage values apply, but the laser gets a -2 to hit modifier and +2 heat, regardless of laser size. If a modified laser rolls an unmodified 2 on the to-hit roll or the LPM is damaged by a critical hit, the LPM will catastrophically fail. This inflicts a 2 point ammunition explosion on the location mounting the LPM. Instead of rolling for critical hits normally, the unit suffers a single automatic critical hit to the first undamaged slot of the laser that uses the LPM.<ref name=iop93/>

If the laser attached to the LPM is damaged via critical hit before the LPM is damaged, the LPM is rendered inert as well. An inert LPM does not explode on a critical hit.

Lasers with LPMs can use [[Targeting Computer]]s, but when firing in pulse mode they cannot use the Targeting Computer for an aimed shot.<ref name=iop93/>

The battle value of a laser with a Laser Pulse Module is 1.15 times the laser's base cost. For example a [[Large Laser]] with an LPM would have a battle value of 141.

The Laser Pulse Module costs 200,000 C-bills.

==Description==
The '''Light PPC''' is a smaller version of the [[Particle Projector Cannon]]. Introduced by the [[Draconis Combine]] in [[3067]], it is often compared with the [[ER medium laser]] as it shares damage, heat, and lack of ammunition dependency. The Light PPC is heavier and more bulky, though it does have a longer range profile, albeit with the deficiency of a minimum range. Overall, it's a light and long-range weapon, but not a replacement for the [[medium laser]] or ER medium laser. Light PPCs cannot be installed on [[ProtoMech]]s.

==Description==
The '''Light Sub-Capital Laser''', also known as the '''SCL/1''', was the smallest of the sub-capital lasers produced by the [[Word of Blake]]. Though it didn't inflict the massive damage of its heavier cousins, it could fire as long as it had power. In addition, the relatively small size of the weapon meant that it could be mounted on even the smallest [[DropShip]] platform, so the Word could put many of these weapons into combat. Individually they might only pack the punch of an [[Autocannon∕10]], but when used ''en masse'' they could easily damage or destroy all but the strongest targets.<ref name="TOp343"/>
 
The fact that a WarShip could mount four of these systems in place of a single [[Naval Laser 35]] made them popular with some WarShip commanders who faced large numbers of [[DropShips]] or [[Pocket WarShip|Pocket WarShips]].

Though they were more responsive to their control systems than their larger cousins, they still had difficulty targeting targets smaller than 500 tons.<ref name="TOp343"/>

## Weapon: Medium laser
### Data Extract Profile
{{InfoBoxWeapon
| image = [[File:Inverted Medium Laser.jpg|191px]]

==Description==
The '''Medium Laser''' is a midrange weapon of the standard type from the [[laser]] class of weaponry.  A [[w:Free-electron laser|free-electron laser]], it is a device that focuses an amplified beam of light on a small surface area. The medium laser uses this intensified beam of light to damage targets by heating them to melting temperatures. The relatively compact size and effectiveness of the Medium Laser attributes its widespread use over many types of combat units, though it is regarded as a "[[Support Laser (Heavy)|Heavy Support Laser]]" when carried by infantry squads or mounted on [[battle armor|powered armor]].{{cn}}

The ubiquitous weapon eventually earned, by [[3068]], a [[Medium Laser (beverage)|cocktail in its name]].

==Models==
Being one of the most common and oldest class of weapon system in use, there are a vast number of manufacturers and factories producing these. This reference should not be considered complete.

=== Allied Technologies Model 2 ===
Manufactured by Allied Technologies, the [[Star League era]] Allied Technologies Model 2 was proven to be a reliable weapon, their standard coil-cooling insulation proving adaptable to the airframes of the company's then parent company [[New Age Systems Incorporated]] aerospace fighters.<ref name=TRO2750p72>''Technical Readout: 2750'', p. 72: "GTHA-500 Gotha"</ref> Examples on the ''Ironsides'' however had an well known energy housing problem that had not been resolved even after three recalls. If the Model 2s mounted on ''Ironsides'' are fired extensively, their power-chain housing could rupture and destructively break their coolant system, violently damaging the internal structure of the fighter's wings at a critical juncture point. While never absolutely proven, this flaw was suspected as the cause of three known pilot deaths when their wings exploded during a dogfight.<ref name=TRO2750p74>''Technical Readout: 2750'', p. 74: "IRN-SD1 Ironsides"</ref>

==Description==
Introduced in [[2609]] by the [[Terran Hegemony]]. '''Pulse lasers''' differ from traditional laser weaponry in that instead of firing one powerful beam, they maintain laser beams fired off in quick succession. While offering an overall increased rate of fire, the heat output also increases accordingly. Pulse lasers increase damage because they allow vaporized armor to dissipate from the location of damage. This allows subsequent pulses to reach the target area without being diffused by the vapor.<ref name="TMp226">''TechManual'', p. 226: "Laser - Pulse Lasers"</ref> Like all pulse lasers, Medium Pulse Lasers are also more accurate than traditional Medium Lasers. However, they have a lower effective range.

'''Medium Pulse Lasers''' are a mix of range and damage, making them useful in most situations. The heat buildup is moderate and the weapon causes good damage, although the Medium Pulse Laser still cannot be constantly discharged for extended periods of time.<ref name="TMp226"/>

While not designated as such in the names, Clan Pulse Lasers have greatly extended range over Inner Sphere models.

== Models ==
The Medium Pulse Laser is manufactured on the following planets:
===14a===
Possibly related to the Series 14a, the 14a is a Clan grade medium pulse laser used on [[Clan Snow Raven]] designs in the [[Dark Age era]].

===Magna 600P===
One of a number of advanced lasers manufactured by the [[Outworlds Alliance]]-based Lushann Industries, the reliable Magna 600P is widely used by [[Federated Suns]]-based [[Robinson Standard BattleWorks]] in numerous designs and refits it constructed in the Civil War era. This superb weapon is so prized that technicians working on 'Mechs factory fitted with them like the OTL-6D ''Ostsol'' have learned to keep an eye on them to avoid other techs "borrowing" them.<ref name=TRJp134>''Technical Readout: Jihad'', p. 134: "OTL-6D Ostsol"</ref>

==Description==
The '''Medium Re-Engineered Laser''' is an advanced-grade energy weapon first developed by the [[Federated Suns]] in the [[thirty-second century|mid thirty-second century]]. The weapon along with its other brethren were originally developed in an attempt to create a hybrid of the [[Clans]]' [[Heavy Laser]] and [[Pulse Laser]]. However, the resulting hybrid resulted in a weapon that neither was able to inflict heavy damage nor improved accuracy noticeable to a pilot.

However, the unexpected results of this experiment was an energy weapon able to defeat reinforced and advanced armor types first fielded during the [[Jihad]] and the later [[Dark Age]].  The weapon when facing other armor types is only able to inflict slightly more damage than a standard model laser, in this case the Medium Laser. Also, the Medium RE Laser also has the same ranges and the standard Medium Laser as well.

==Description==
The '''Medium Sub-Capital Laser''', also known as the '''SCL/2''', was the middleweight of the Sub-Capital Lasers produced by the [[Word of Blake]]. When it hit a target, it was like being hit by a [[Barracuda]] missile. Even more frighteningly, the SCL/2 wasn't dependent on ammunition so it could fire as long as the [[Pocket WarShip]] had power.<ref name="TOp343"/>

Though they were more responsive to their control systems than their larger cousins, they still had difficulty targeting targets smaller than 500 tons.<ref name="TOp343"/>

==Description==
The '''Medium Variable-Speed Pulse Laser''' is, as its name suggests, the midsize version of the [[variable-speed pulse laser]] family of weapons. At twice the weight of a standard [[medium pulse laser]], a [[Word of Blake]] [[MechWarrior (pilot)|MechWarrior]] can inflict up to fifty percent more damage with this weapon, as compared to its standard [[pulse laser]] counterpart.

==Description==
X-Pulse Lasers were an attempt by [[Inner Sphere]] technicians to increase the range of [[pulse laser]]s to compete with the longer ranges of similar [[Clan]] models. X-pulse lasers are not an entirely new weapon per se, but rather an upgrade that is separately added to the weapon for an additional cost. X-pulse lasers also build up tremendous amounts of heat, but this has been considered by technicians as a necessary drawback.<ref name="MTRp82">''Maximum Tech, Revised'', p. 82: "X-Pulse Laser"</ref><ref name="TOp321">''Tactical Operations'', p. 321: "X-Pulse Laser"</ref>

Like their standard [[Medium Pulse Laser|pulse counterparts]], '''Medium X-Pulse Lasers''' are the workhorse of the X-pulse laser series, and though they build large amounts of extra heat, they boast the same range as a standard [[medium laser]]. They also offer the improved targeting of the standard pulse laser.<ref name="TOp321"/>

==Description==
The '''Micro Pulse Laser''' was designed by [[Clan Smoke Jaguar]] (alongside the [[ER Micro Laser]]) primarily for use on their [[ProtoMech|ProtoMechs]]. It has since spread to all the [[Clans]]. It has been used on several Battle Armor systems.  Like the Small Pulse Laser, this model can be set to a burst fire mode designed to eliminate infantry.<ref name=TMp226/>

== Models ==
Since the Micro Pulse Laser is exclusively a [[Clan]] weapon, there are no [[Inner Sphere]] manufacturers or models. This also means that there are no X-Pulse, ER Pulse or Variable Speed versions of this weapon. Additionally, it has not yet been modified to serve as a [[Laser Anti-Missile System]], like the Small Pulse Laser has.

## Weapon: Naval Laser
### Data Extract Profile
[[File:Naval Laser.jpg|right|thumb|Naval Laser (Capital Laser)]]

==Description==
The '''Naval Laser''' ('''NL''') systems used aboard [[WarShips]] and planetary defense systems ([[SDS]]) are massive versions of the standard laser systems used on [['Mechs]] and other [[combat vehicles]]. They inflict damage via a focused beam of light that melts or overheats the target. The systems used by WarShips are massive, often costing as much as a 'Mech. In addition, their huge size makes them impractical for targeting small craft or [[aerospace fighter]]s; Like their [[Naval PPC]] and [[Naval Autocannon]] counterparts, they can only target sub-500 ton craft with extreme difficulty.<ref name="TRO:2750p122">''Technical Readout: 2750'', p. 122, "Naval Autocannon"</ref> For this reason WarShips typically carry standard sized weapons to defend against attacks by small craft. Their main advantage over Naval PPCs is their good damage to heat ratio; Two NL35's can fire on a target and inflict as much damage as a Light NPPC. Their advantage over Naval Autocannon is their lack of ammunition dependence. In addition, Naval Lasers were often used in the Terran Hegemony SDS systems to defend important worlds. In [[3060]], several lasers mounted on tower platforms were used to defend [[Tranquil]]. They proved to be capable of taking down an orbiting ''[[Union]]''-class DropShip with no more than two shots, although the targeting was slow enough to allow partial deployment of forces via [[Drop Pod]]s.

As of [[3067]], there are no known [[:Category:Lasers#Extended Range Lasers|Extended Range]], [[:Category:Lasers#Pulse Lasers|Pulse]], or [[:Category:Lasers#Heavy Lasers|Heavy]] versions of Naval Laser systems.{{cn}}

Like their ground-based counterparts, the Naval Laser systems can be grouped into three families based roughly on size:

* [[Naval Laser 35]] ([[NL35]])
* [[Naval Laser 45]] ([[NL45]])
* [[Naval Laser 55]] ([[NL55]])

==Description==
The '''Naval Laser 55''' ('''NL55''') class is the [[WarShip]] capital weapon equivalent of a [[Large Laser]]. It is cheaper and generates less heat than a [[Light NPPC]], and offers 85% of the damage potential.<ref name="TOp333"/>

## Weapon: Particle Projector Cannon
### Data Extract Profile
{{Update Needed|[[Shrapnel (magazine)#Issue 4|Shrapnel #4]]}}
{{About|the powerful energy weapon|the infamous alcoholic drink|PPC (beverage)}}
{{InfoBoxWeapon
| image = [[File:Inverted PPC.jpg|191px]]

| Tech Base = [[Inner Sphere]] (IS)

==Description==
The '''Particle Projector Cannon''' (or '''PPC''') is an energy weapon, firing a concentrated stream of protons or ions at a target with damage resulting from both thermal and kinetic energy.<ref name=TM_233>''TechManual'', p. 233: "Particle Projector Cannon (PPC)"</ref>  Despite being an energy weapon, it does produce recoil. The lethality of the weapon rivals that of higher-caliber [[autocannon]]s; just three shots from a PPC will vaporize nearly two tons of [[standard armor|standard military-grade armor]]. During the Star League era, multiple hits from certain PPC models were known to scramble electronics, until the SLDF introduced dampers. These proliferated to the armies of the Great Houses and beyond during the outbreak of the Succession Wars. The ion beam also extends to much farther ranges than autocannon fire, though PPCs generate large amounts of waste heat, the earlier prototype versions (produced between [[2439]] and [[2460]]) even more so.

PPCs are equipped with a [[field inhibitor]] to prevent feedback that could damage the firing unit's electronic systems.  This inhibitor degrades the performance of the weapon at close ranges of less than 90 meters.  Particularly daring warriors have been known to disengage the inhibitor and risk damage to their own machine when a target is at close range.

===Anderson Armaments Particle Cannon PPC 12===
Considered by some observers as the best weapon produced by [[Anderson Armaments]], when linked to the [[Garret GRNDTRK 9]] [[targeting and tracking system]] the weapon would prove to be particularly deadly.<ref name=TRO3025p68/> During the [[Third Succession War]] the weapon was considered more desirable than the four-legged ''Scorpion''s that came equipped with it, with many examples being removed and repurposed for use on more favored 'Mech designs instead.<ref name=TRO3025CE_A11>''Technical Readout: 3025 Centennial Edition'', Appendix A p. A-11 :"Scorpion"</ref>

===Parti-Kill Heavy Cannon===
Unlike other PPCs, the Parti-Kill Heavy Cannon does not use an energy collection capacitor or similar chamber, instead using a series of magnetic collection bottles that gather plasma straight from the 'Mech or vehicle's [[fusion reactor]]. The resulting energies are then channeled through a larger magnetic bottle and released from the cannon as an energy "shell" that is unstable at ranges of less than ninety meters and loses cohesion and disintegrates at 540 meters.<ref name=TRO3026>''Technical Readout: 3026'', pp. 64–65</ref><ref name=TRO3026R>''Technical Readout: 3026 Revised'', pp. 68–69</ref><ref name=TRO3039>''Technical Readout: 3039'', pp. 74–75</ref>

===Tiegart===
The Tiegart Particle Projection Cannon provides similar performance to the Donal PPC while being ten percent smaller. Unlike the more common Donal however, even during the Star League era Tiegart replacement parts were more scarce with fire control suffering greatly without a steady supply of parts, even with several redundancy circuits providing backup if needed.<ref name=TRO2750p48>''Technical Readout: 2750'', pp. 48–49</ref> Production of the Tiegart PPC ended during the destruction of the early Succession War with designs utlizing the more compact PPC being reworked to use the more common Donal PPCs. <ref name=TRO3025rp102>''Technical Readout: 3025 Revised'', pp. 102–103</ref>

==Variants==
* '''Prototype''': The ''[[Mackie]]'' used an older prototype for the Particle Projector Cannon, seeing as it was equipped with one before the weapon is even introduced. According to the rules in ''[[Interstellar Operations]]'' regarding prototype energy weapons, the primary difference between the prototype and the mass production models is a considerably increased heat output (around 50% higher).
* '''Improved PPC''': Developed by the [[Clans]] shortly after the [[Operation EXODUS|Exodus]], the only differences between the Improved PPC and a normal PPC would be that the mass is only 6000 kilograms, and that the weapon only occupies 2 Critical Slots. All other performance characteristics remain same, and it would  go on to become obsolete when the Clans perfected the [[Extended Range Particle Projector Cannon|ER-PPC]]. This weapon was also described in ''Interstellar Operations.''
* '''Light and Heavy PPCs''': Developed by the [[Draconis Combine]] in [[3067]], the [[Light PPC|Light]] model significantly cut down on the size and weight of the weapon at the expense of damage output, and the [[Heavy PPC|Heavy]] model increased the damage output at the expense of increased heat and mass.
* '''Snub-Nose PPC''': By modifying the PPC to have a much shorter barrel and plasma focus, the [[Snub-Nose PPC]] sacrifices its maximum effective range (especially since damage starts to drop significantly as distance increases), but makes up for it by lacking a minimum range and being much better suited for close-quarters engagements. Additionally, it is actually easier for some mechs to carry these things when a regular PPC wouldn't fit.
* '''ER PPC''': The [[Extended Range Particle Projector Cannon]] has a much further maximum range than its ancestor, along with the additional benefit of not having a minimum range. The downside to this, however, is the increased heat output. Though the weapon became Lostech for use in the Inner Sphere, the Clans never lost the technology, and managed to make improvements.

===Apocryphal Variants===
{{ApocryphalContentStart}}
''From [[MechAssault]]:''
*; [[Plasma PPC|'''Plasma PPC''']]: An ancient weapon reintroduced by the Word of Blake, this variant of the PPC offers a greater rate of fire, but lower damage per shot.<ref name="MS2ump20">''MechAssault 2:Lone Wolf user manual'', p. 20, "Energy Weapons"</ref><ref name="MSPMump25">''MechAssault: Phantom War user manual'', p. 25, "'Mech Energy Weapons"</ref>

''From [[MechWarrior Online Legends]]:''
*; Plasma PPC: This incarnation of the Plasma PPC resembles a Clan version of the Light PPC.<ref name=MWO>''MechWarrior Online Legends'' (video game)</ref>

''From [[MechWarrior 5: Mercenaries#Solaris_Showdown|''MechWarrior 5: Solaris Showdown'' Expansion Pack]]:'' 
*;'''PPC-X''' : (Effectively an [[LB-X Autocannon|LB-X]] version of the PPC.) Particle Projector Cannon equipped with an ion separator that fires eight high-energy ion bolts. Creates multiple brief disruption fields within 90m of the impact.<ref name=MW5SS>''MechWarrior 5: Mercenaries, Solaris Showdown''</ref>


{{ApocryphalContentEnd}}

==See Also==
* [[PPC Capacitor]]: This piece of equipment can be added to PPCs in order to increase the damage output, but on top of being slow to charge, this capacitor is prone to causing explosions. Some 'Mechs that are desperate to save weight will drop their full-sized PPC for a Light PPC and an added Capacitor.
* [[Blue Shield Particle Field Dampener]] (BSPFD): A system that was developed in order to reduce the damage output from incoming PPCs, the BSPFD isn't useful for much else and can occupy vital space in a 'Mech.

| Long Range = 16-25
| Tons = 8
| Critical Slots = 6
| Ammo Per Ton = n/a
| Cost (unloaded) = 750,000
| Ammo Cost (per ton) = n/a

}}


The '''RISC Hyper Laser''' is an experimental heavy energy weapon derived from the Clans' [[Improved Heavy Laser]] family.

==Description==
Hyper Lasers are the ultimate development in heavy laser technology. [[Republic Institute of Strategic Combat]] engineers based the Hyper Laser on Clan heavy laser technology, and created a weapon surpassing even those weapons in sheer range and destructive power.

RISC Hyper Lasers push the limits of modern laser shielding and chemistry to achieve their sheer power. Work on these weapons started in 3133, with the first prototypes being fielded a year later. These prototypes quickly displayed a tendency towards explosive malfunctions, owing to the sheer thermal stress generated by the weapon.

This flaw reared its head up during one of the many "false flag" operations conducted by the [[Republic Armed Forces]], when a ''[[Savage Wolf (Mad Cat Mk IV)|Mad Cat Mk IV]]'' equipped with these new Hyper Lasers suffered several malfunctions and subsequent explosions of its weapons during combat. This would eventually lead to the 'Mech's defeat and destruction. This incident, and others like it, may have contributed to the project's eventual cancellation.

One of the first battles featuring this weapon pitted a Mad Cat Mk IV against four '[[Mech]]s, destroying two of them, a ''[[Catapult]]'' and a ''[[Jackalope]]'', though the 'Mech exploded once it was defeated.

==Description==
The '''Small Laser''' is a [[w:Free-electron laser|free-electron laser]] which focuses a beam of light on targets to cause damage.<ref name="ER:2750p99">''Era Report: 2750'', p. 99: "Laser Systems"</ref> The compact size of the Small Laser allows it to be equipped and used efficiently on practically anything; however the reduced firepower means that Small Lasers are not usually used on heavier combat devices such as 'Mechs or tanks. [[Small Laser (Battle Armor)|Small lasers]] can also be mounted on [[battle armor]] or used as an infantry support weapon by conventional infantry, where it is referred to as a "[[Support Laser]]."<ref name="TMp226+">''TechManual'', pp. 226–227: "Laser - Standard Laser"</ref><ref name="TMp291"/>

The ubiquitous weapon eventually earned a [[Small Laser (beverage)|cocktail in its name]].<ref name=S21VotS>''Shrapnel Issue 21'': "Voices of the Sphere: What Your Drink Says About You"</ref>

The Small Laser is manufactured on the following planets:

==Description==
Introduced in [[2609]] by the [[Terran Hegemony]], pulse lasers differ from traditional laser weaponry in that instead of firing one powerful beam, they maintain laser beams fired off in quick succession. While offering an overall increased rate of fire, the heat output also increases accordingly.  Pulse lasers increase damage because they allow vaporized armor to dissipate from the location of damage.  This allows subsequent pulses to reach the target area without being diffused by the vapor.<ref name="TMp226"/> Pulse lasers are also more accurate than their traditional counterparts.

'''Small Pulse Lasers''', being the smallest pulse laser variant, have a very limited range. Despite that, they also have a much smaller heat buildup for their damage output compared to other pulse lasers, which makes them an excellent short-ranged support weapon. Due to this the Small Pulse Laser is also a popular option for [[battle armor]].

Unlike other [[Inner Sphere]] Pulse Laser models, the Small Pulse Laser has the same range as a traditional Small Laser.

While not designated as such in the names, [[Clan]] Pulse Lasers have greatly extended range over [[Inner Sphere]] models.<ref name="TMp226"/>

== Models ==
The Small Pulse Laser is manufactured on the following planets:
===Chi Series===
Its manufacture stretching from the [[Golden Century]]<ref name=TRGCp32/> to at least the [[ilClan era]],<ref name=RGv1Cp142/> the Chi Series is a Clan-tech Small Pulse Laser used by multiple Clans in the [[Clan Homeworlds|Homeworlds]] and Inner Sphere.

### Data Extract Profile

| Damage = 4<ref name="FM:3145p256-257">''Field Manual: 3145'', pp. 256-257, "Dark Age Weapons And Equipment Combat Data"</ref>
| Minimum Range = N/A

| Critical Slots = 1<ref name="FM:3145p256-257"/>
| Cost (unloaded) = 25,000<ref name="FM:3145p256-257"/>
| BV (2.0) = 14<ref name=errata2>[http://bg.battletech.com/forums/index.php?topic=33062.msg1171219#msg1171219 Dev-level errata January 17, 2016]</ref>
}}

==Description==
The '''Small Re-Engineered Laser''' is an offshoot of experiments to mate a Clan [[Heavy Laser]] with a [[Pulse Laser]]. Though the experiments were unsuccessful, the resulting Re-Engineered Laser family has the unique ability to ignore the effects of [[Ferro-Lamellor]], [[Hardened Armor|Hardened]], and [[Reflective Armor]]. This allows the Small Re-Engineered Laser to inflict its full damage on units protected by those armor types.<ref name="FM:3145p244/>

The Small Re-Engineered Laser is the smallest member of the Re-Engineered Laser family. Like all Re-Engineered Lasers, the pulse cycle is so short that it only has a -1 to-hit modifier.<ref name=errata1/> It is compatible with [[Targeting Computer]]s. Aside from its unique armor-penetrating properties, it behaves like a standard [[Small Laser]].
 
== Models ==

No name was given for manufacturer the Small Re-Engineered Laser carried on the [[Fire Falcon#Alternate Configurations|''Fire Falcon'' R variant]]. The lasers on the ''Fire Falcon'' R are Inner Sphere based making the ''Fire Falcon'' a mixed technology Clan Omni 'Mech. As of 1 Jan 2024 there are no other examples of Small Re-Engineered Lasers in the BattleTech Universe.

==Description==
X-Pulse lasers are an attempt by Inner Sphere technicians to increase the range of pulse lasers to compete with the longer ranges of similar Clan models. X-Pulse lasers are not a new weapon ''per se'', but an upgrade that is separately added to the weapon for an additional cost. X-Pulse lasers also build up tremendous heat but technicians have considered this as a necessary drawback.<ref name="TOp321">''Tactical Operations'', p. 321: "X-Pulse Laser"</ref>

The '''small X-Pulse laser''' is the smallest of these weapons. It has good damage potential for its heat and boasts almost as much range as an Inner Sphere [[medium pulse laser]] or a Clan [[small pulse laser]]. Like all pulse lasers, the higher rate of fire allows for increased accuracy.

==Description==
The '''Snub-Nose Particle Projector Cannon''' is to a [[particle projector cannon]] as a sawed-off shotgun is to a shotgun. The Snub-Nose PPC was designed and distributed by the [[Star League]] to the [[SLDF]] during the reign of [[First Lord]] [[Jonathan Cameron]], in particular its [[Star League Defense Force Royal Divisions|Royal Divisions]]. The design saw limited use and disappeared during the [[Succession Wars (History)|Succession Wars]] until it was rediscovered by the [[Draconis Combine]].<ref name="TMp234"/> 

The snub-nose PPC is slightly lighter than a standard PPC, but its energy discharge trails off considerably faster, leading to shorter range and reduced damage capacity over range. An interesting side benefit is the dramatically increased short range factor, longer than almost any other weapon.

==Models==
The snub-nose PPC is manufactured on the following planets:

===Ceres Arms Thrasher===
The Ceres Arms Thrasher is noted as providing excellent accuracy at 270 meters with a maximum standard engagement range of 405 meters,<ref name=TRO3085p58/> an optimum range band that makes the Thrasher ideal for urban combat.<ref name=TRO3085p247/>

### Data Extract Profile

| image =

| Damage = 0

| Critical Slots = 5<ref name=FM:3145p257/>
| Cost (unloaded) = 800,000<ref name=FM:3145p257/>
| BV (2.0) = 488<ref name="FM:3145p256"/>
}}

==Description==
Similar in concept to the SLDF’s failed [[Centurion Weapon System]], the '''Tight-Stream-Electro-Magnetic-Pulse Cannon''' (TSEMP) is a directed energy weapon that was created by the Republic of the Sphere. It's based on the [[Word of Blake]]'s [[BattleMech Taser]] system, but doesn't require the cabling and capacitors used by the Blakist system. The TSEMP Cannon is a multishot version of the TSEMP One-shot weapon. When fired at a powered combat unit of less than 200 tons, the TSEMP can force the unit to shut down completely or interfere with targeting systems, sensors, and other electronics equipment. Special armors and defenses like the [[Blue Shield PFD]] have no effect on this effect.<ref name="FM:3145p245"/>

The ability to shut down an opponent is useful, but there are several drawbacks. The TSEMP Cannon uses capacitors to charge and if these are hit by hostile weapons fire they will explode in the same way as a [[Gauss rifle]]. The unit mounting the TSEMP Cannon suffers from sensor and electronic system interference every time it fires the TSEMP. The capacitors used to charge the Cannon also require time to recharge between shots, so an operator will have to wait ten seconds between shots. Furthermore, the TSEMP Cannon's power requirements mean it can only be used by combat units with a fission or fusion engine.<ref name="FM:3145p245"/>

Though the Republic developed the technology, they sold it to the other powers of the Inner Sphere.
